# K1c slice 4 evidence

The same independent reviewer issued S4 PASS under qa-loop, qa-tests and
code-quality-qa after independently checking the final code, courts, raw logs,
exits and scoped lint paths. It ran no builds and made no edits. Query commands use
WORTH_IMPLEMENTATION_BUILD_ID=11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111.
Counts below are copied from completed runs, including failed attempts. Full local
logs remain under target/k1c-logs; this document preserves their diagnostic output.
A test count is not inferred from a compilation result or an empty filter.

## Scope and independent evidence

Native retains actual field-bound frames on Source loans, projected fields, demanded
preimages and original-output material. Correction reattaches those same Native
frames through the existing pre-publication domain seal. No free-ID Query resolver,
parallel byte table, Store dependency, AspectShape form or new hashing owner was added.
The only typed generic work left in original-output acquisition is witness dispatch;
Native certification and attachment traversal are concrete.

The generic provider uses a constant conservative candidate key. A separate literal
integer-list full scan decides expected exact membership. Frozen little-endian frames
and Python hashlib/OpenSSL vectors decide expected bytes and identities independently
of the production writer and Native hasher. Native suspension uses genuine completion
and restoration custody, zero caller attachments, a proposed-only observation counter,
and selected physical absence. Wrong route tickets cannot enter a field callback.
Transported introduced records cannot stand in for complete historical custody.
Original-field capture measured Work thresholds 18,959 / 19,169 / 23,633 for one aspect,
128 aspects, and a 256-byte aspect identifier respectively; every failed threshold
probe releases original capture memory. These are measured invoice thresholds, not
production API parameters or a claim of S5 cost qualification.

## Review findings closed on inspection

- Every retained preimage/output entry pays and polls its original request, including non-content entries.
- Swallowed Source projection errors latch the original typed refusal.
- Native aspect/field lookup invoices cover actual collection height and key bytes.
- Duplicate Source corruption retains ContentInconsistency through erased/attempt/commit receivers.
- Wrong present frame identity refuses EncodingInconsistency, never optional absence.
- Execution stops normalize through the existing relational mapper; semantic content errors remain distinct.
- ContentInconsistency is explicitly terminal in both query recovery classifiers.
- Historical certification and retention moved out of generic engine bodies.
- Pure mapping tests use literal receiving enums and resource limits.
- Suspension checks proposed content separately from committed content.

The final public run has 19 passing tests (327 filtered), including the Source
full-scan oracles, undo/reapplication, original-output closure and exact initial
RootUnavailable after checkpoint. A genuine authored edit cold-stages before/proposed
Source while an unchanged recovered row still participates. No immediate maintained
open/rebuild or restored live correction history is claimed.

Final clippy exits remain 101 solely for untouched debt: Native has 1 diagnostic
(payload.rs let_and_return); its direct court has 4 diagnostics in untouched
managed-source work-account/nested courts; Query has 64 diagnostics in untouched
Decl/Host document-history/workflow courts. A complete scoped union of dirty,
untracked and origin/master...HEAD paths has zero overlap with these final error
paths. All caused lint errors were fixed. No suppression, baseline/config change
or unrelated repair was made. The same reviewer independently verified this
exception under AGENTS and found no remaining required code/test finding.

## Final staging and documentation checks

The initial cached diff check exited 2 because copied diagnostic excerpts retained
trailing whitespace. Trimming only that whitespace fixed the document; subsequent
cached checks 2 and 3 both exited 0. The final cached check follows this status update.
The initial status-update Python script also failed before changing files because
PowerShell wrote an en dash as a non-UTF-8 byte. It was rewritten as UTF-8; there
was no code, test or publication effect.
